# EARTH KNOWLEDGE SOURCE REGISTRY
## War Room OS — Global Knowledge Infrastructure Discovery (Verified 2026)

**Mission:** A verified, machine-accessible registry of Earth's knowledge infrastructure across all major domains — software, security, medicine, biology, earth science, human history, government, economics, science, geography, and general knowledge — searched GLOBALLY, with deliberate coverage of Africa, Asia, Latin America, the Middle East, and Oceania, including local-language sources.

**Verification standard:** Every source was checked against official documentation and, wherever possible, live-probed during August 2026. No source, endpoint, credential, or signup URL was invented. Sources confirmed dead or degraded are marked **DISCONTINUED** / **DEGRADED** with their replacement where one exists. An ordinary website is never called an API — search-only resources are labeled `SEARCH INTERFACE`.

**Companion registry:** African-focused sources live in the separate verified registry `war_room_africa_api_registry.md` (~110 sources). This registry cross-references rather than duplicates them.

---

## FIELD SCHEMA (34 verification fields per source block)

1 Name · 2 Organization · 3 Country/Region · 4 Coverage · 5 Domains · 6 Website · 7 Documentation URL · 8 API Base / Access Point · 9 Access Mechanism · 10 Protocol · 11 Authentication · 12 Key Required? · 13 Account Required? · 14 Official Signup URL · 15 Credential Process · 16 Free/Freemium/Paid · 17 Rate Limits · 18 Formats · 19 Bulk Download? · 20 Historical Data? · 21 Full Content vs Metadata · 22 Search Capability · 23 License · 24 Commercial Use · 25 Attribution · 26 Update Frequency · 27 2026 Operational Status · 28 Reliability Tier (A–E) · 29 Integration Difficulty · 30 Environment Variable (`WARROOM_*`) · 31 Example Request · 32 Limitations · 33 Unique Information · 34 Overlap

## AUTHORITY TIER LEGEND

| Tier | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **A** | Official government / scientific primary source (NIST, WHO, USGS, national registries) |
| **B** | Major academic institution or international organization (EMBL-EBI, Cornell, OECD) |
| **C** | Established nonprofit / open-data institution (GBIF, iNaturalist, Wikimedia) |
| **D** | Commercial provider (VirusTotal, Shodan, DrugBank) |
| **E** | Community / open-source project (GloBI, Rosetta Code) |

## ACCESS TYPE VOCABULARY

`REST` · `GRAPHQL` · `SPARQL` · `OAI-PMH` · `IIIF` · `SDMX` · `ODATA` · `STAC` · `TAP` · `WMS/WFS` · `PXWEB` · `BULK DOWNLOAD` · `FEED` (RSS/Atom/CSAF/MISP) · `REPOSITORY` (git) · `SEARCH INTERFACE` (no machine API — negative finding) · `COMMERCIAL-GATED`

## EVIDENCE CLASSIFICATIONS (preserved from sources — never flatten)

`TRAD` recorded traditional use / pharmacopoeia record (NOT clinical proof) · `EXP` experimental/literature evidence · `PRED` computational prediction · `NOMEN` nomenclatural only · `CLIN` clinical · Threat-intel distinctions (confirmed-exploited vs predicted vs community-reported) are likewise preserved per source.

## REGISTRY CATEGORY MAP (25 categories)

| # | Category | Primary content |
|---|---|---|
| 01 | General Web Knowledge & Reference | Wikimedia ecosystem, crawls, archives, dictionaries, knowledge graphs, entity/identifier systems |
| 02 | Software & Coding | Package registries, code platforms, code search, Q&A, standards docs, lifecycle data |
| 03 | Bugs, Patches & Software History | Vulnerability databases, advisories, issue trackers, vendor feeds, EOL |
| 04 | Cybersecurity / CVE / Malware / Threat Intel | CVE ecosystem, ATT&CK, IOC platforms, sandboxes, phishing, CERT feeds |
| 05 | Human Anatomy & Terminologies | UMLS, SNOMED CT, LOINC, ontology services |
| 06 | Diseases & Clinical Knowledge | Literature, trials, classifications, rare diseases, guidelines, surveillance |
| 07 | Pharmaceuticals & Medicines | Regulators, drug nomenclature, bioactivity, interactions, PGx |
| 08 | Traditional & Indigenous Medicine | TCM, Ayurveda, ethnobotany, pharmacopoeias, traditional-knowledge governance |
| 09 | Genetics & Molecular Biology | Sequence archives, genes, proteins, structures, pathways, interactions |
| 10 | Biology & Biodiversity | Occurrences, taxonomy, conservation, traits, interactions, barcodes |
| 11 | Earth & Environmental | Weather, climate, elevation, bathymetry, environmental monitoring |
| 12 | Human Origins & Archaeology | Excavation data, epigraphy, gazetteers, chronology |
| 13 | World History & Primary Sources | Historical datasets, classical texts, canonical corpora, persecution archives |
| 14 | Government & Law | Legislation, courts, sanctions, elections, public spending |
| 15 | Economics & Finance | Company registers, identifiers, central banks, financial filings |
| 16 | Academic Research | Citation graphs, OA aggregators, preprints, discipline archives |
| 17 | Patents & Intellectual Property | Patent offices, trademarks, IP registers |
| 18 | Maps / GIS / Satellite / Earth Observation | Base maps, geocoding, imagery, national mapping agencies |
| 19 | Space & Astronomy | Satellite catalogs, orbital data, space weather, sky surveys |
| 20 | Engineering & Technology | Standards bodies, materials databases, engineering references |
| 21 | Archives / Libraries / Museums | National libraries, aggregators, archives, museum collections |
| 22 | News & Historical News | Newspaper archives, press APIs, historical periodicals |
| 23 | Statistics / Census / Demographics | National statistical offices, census, population |
| 24 | International Organizations | UN system, development banks, humanitarian data |
| 25 | Specialized & Rare + Regional | Unique/niche datasets and non-Western regional infrastructure |

# EARTH KNOWLEDGE SOURCE REGISTRY — PART 1 (Categories 01–04)

Integrated from verified source reports: earth_kb_wave2/15_general_web_reference.md (Category 01), earth_kb_wave1/01_software_coding.md (Category 02), earth_kb_wave1/02_bugs_patches.md (Category 03), earth_kb_wave1/03_cybersecurity.md (Category 04). Pure reorganization — no sources added, removed, or re-verified.

### Category notes

#### Cross-reference only (registered elsewhere; do not duplicate)
- Stack Exchange API + dumps — Wave 1 registered (api.stackexchange.com, free, CC BY-SA; dumps on archive.org). OVERLAP w/ Reddit block above. → Full block in CATEGORY 02 (#17).
- Library of Congress APIs (loc.gov JSON API + SRU) — registered in US registry; cross-ref for identifier blocks (VIAF links LCCN).
- Crossref/DOI/Handle — scholarly identifiers wave; ISNI/ORCID/ROR here link into it.
- OpenSanctions — left to government/sanctions agent (overlap note only).
- Google Books API (https://www.googleapis.com/books/v1/volumes?q=, key optional, free) — likely covered in books/registry wave; note as gap-filler here if unclaimed.
- ISBNdb (isbndb.com, paid REST, from ~$15/mo) — commercial books metadata; note only.
- GeoNames full detail — geo agent; brief block 28 above.

#### Status notes (2026)
- DISCONTINUED: Bing Search APIs (retired 2025-08-11, decommissioned; replacement = Grounding with Bing Search in Azure AI Agents, LLM-mediated not raw search). GRID (org IDs) — long discontinued, successor ROR (#23).
- DEGRADED: Reddit API (OAuth-mandatory, approval-gated since late 2025, unauthenticated .json 403s since ~May 2026; commercial ~$0.24/1k calls). ConceptNet public API (transient 5xx observed; data aging, OMCS feeds ended). Glosbe (403 anti-bot on API probe; use UA/header workarounds cautiously). Wikibase Cloud (open beta, queryservice incident history). ISNI SRU legacy host isni.oclc.org 404 — re-verify current base URL from isni.org tech docs.
- CHANGED: Google KG Search split Standard/Enterprise editions Aug 2025 (API still alive). Data Commons now requires API key (self-serve apikeys.datacommons.org) + GCP-hosted MCP endpoint launched Feb 2026. VIAF: still OPERATIONAL at viaf.org under OCLC in 2026 despite years of transition rumors; treat as monitor-status. Wikipedia rest_v1 migrating to api.wikimedia.org core REST v1 — build against both.
- VERIFIED ALIVE (live probe 200): Common Crawl index, MusicBrainz WS/2, DBLP, ROR, ORCID public API, DBpedia SPARQL, YAGO SPARQL, Kaikki dumps, Kiwix catalog. Wikimedia/archive.org/Google hosts unreachable from probe sandbox (network restriction), but corroborated OPERATIONAL via Feb-2026-dated docs/third-party evidence.

#### Gaps
1. Encyclopaedia Britannica: NO public API confirmed (website/apps only, SEARCH INTERFACE) — commercial licensing by contact only; not machine-integrable as open source.
2. Arabic dictionaries: no open machine-readable standard found comparable to JMdict/CC-CEDICT — candidates (Almaany, ArabWordNet v4 on GitHub community builds, e.g. Salah-Sal/arabic-wordnet-v4 translating OEWN synsets) are Tier E/unofficial. Arabic lexical gap is real.
3. Pushshift successor landscape (Arctic Shift, PullPush, Academic Torrents) unverified here — needs a dedicated probe pass if Reddit history is required.
4. Chinese knowledge graphs (Baidu Baike, CN-DBpedia, Zhishi.me) and Russian (ruWikidata is fine; but no verified Russian encyclopedic API) — CN-DBpedia API accessibility from outside CN unverified; flag for Asia agent.
5. Korean/Japanese web search APIs (Naver Search API — official, free 25k/day, exists but unverified in this pass; recommend follow-up), Japanese Wikipedia is covered via MediaWiki API.
6. Wordnik API (wordnik.com, key, free tier) — legacy but alive; low priority.
7. IIIF/OAI-PMH for general reference content lives with library/archive agents (LOC, DPLA, Europeana) — out of scope here.

### Category notes

#### Key 2026 status notes / discontinued
- **Sourcegraph public/free code search: DISCONTINUED** (proprietary since 2024; enterprise-paid only; Cody free/pro killed July 2025). Replacement: grep.app + GitHub code search + SWH.
- **libraries.io: DISCONTINUED** (Tidelift sunset) → successor **ecosyste.ms** (#7). (Note: wave1 file 02 registered a libraries.io block in still-OPERATIONAL-but-ownership-transition status — see Category 03 #18; treat as monitor-status.)
- **search.cpan.org**: long dead → MetaCPAN (#5) is sole interface.
- **PyPI XML-RPC API**: deprecated/removed → JSON API only.
- **Debian anonymous rsync of full archive**: restricted → use mirrors/sources.debian.org API/UDD.
- **Rosetta Code**: alive but moved to Miraheze after instability; medium reliability.

---

### Category notes

#### Key answers to the mission's verification questions
- **libraries.io**: ALIVE (verified site visit). Free API w/ account, 60 req/min. Tidelift is being acquired by Sonar (banner live) — watch for changes. Vulnerability data now paywalled behind Tidelift; data is scraped/unvalidated. (Note: this conflicts with the Category 02 report's "libraries.io DISCONTINUED → ecosyste.ms" note — treat libraries.io as OPERATIONAL-but-monitor, ecosyste.ms as successor-in-waiting.)
- **Snyk**: not verified via official docs in this session — do NOT list as integration-ready. Known facts to re-verify: API at api.snyk.io (docs: apidocs.snyk.io), token auth, free tier exists; vuln DB API is largely commercial. **Treat as gated/commercial; verify before wiring.**
- **pyup/Safety DB**: partially verified — `safety` CLI pivoted to Safety Platform API (data.safetycli.com); legacy open safety-db repo (github.com/pyupio/safety-db) still exists but the premium CVSS data is paid. **Freemium, Tier D — low priority vs OSV.**

#### Gaps & notes
1. **No source found for true "breaking changes / regression knowledge"** beyond issue trackers + deps.dev graphs. Renovate data (Renovate) and changelog aggregation have no public API; semver-compat datasets are academic only. This remains a genuine infrastructure gap.
2. RustSec advisory-db (github.com/RustSec/advisory-db, git clone, CC0) and PyPA advisory-database (github.com/pypa/advisory-database, CC-BY-4.0) exist as free git repos but are fully mirrored into OSV — integrate via OSV unless ecosystem-specific depth needed.
3. MITRE cvelistV5 git repo (github.com/CVEProject/cvelistV5) = cheapest bulk CVE mirror; cveawg.mitre.org API is CNA-workflow oriented — recommended as bulk backfill, NVD for query. (CVE Services API full block in Category 04 #2.)
4. Non-US coverage note: this domain is inherently US-vendor-dominated; regional additions worth a follow-up: JVN (Japan, jvn.jp RSS/API — full block in Category 04 #25), CNVD/CNNVD (China, no open API), CERT-FR/ANSSI JSON advisories (cert.ssi.gouv.fr — open JSON feeds; full block in Category 04 #24), BSI CERT-Bund (Germany — full block in Category 04 #26), CIRCL Vulnerability-Lookup (Luxembourg, open API; MISP/CIRCL feeds in Category 04 #23) if War Room needs non-English advisory corpora.

### Category notes

#### Status notes on mission-named items (not separate blocks)
- **Packet Storm Security**: RSS feeds DECOMMISSIONED 2025-10-23 (official notice packetstorm.news/help/view/31). Replacement: paid **Threat Intelligence Feed (API)** with dev API for migration — Tier D, paid, signup via packetstorm contact. No free machine access remains.
- **Feodo Tracker**: feeds currently EMPTY by design (Emotet 2021 + Operation Endgame 2024 takedowns; official FAQ). Official replacement recommendation: Spamhaus Botnet Controller List (BCL, commercial). Do not integrate expecting data.
- **PhishTank**: online but new-user registration disabled since 2020 (2026 OSINT graveyard report; ML redesign underway). Dumps `data.phishtank.com/data/online-valid.{csv,json,xml}` updated hourly; unauthenticated limited to a few downloads/day; existing app keys still work. DEGRADED — treat as legacy.
- **OpenPhish**: `openphish.com/feed.txt` free community feed listed historically in MISP defaults; current free-feed availability NOT verified 2026 (site pushes premium feed). Mark UNVERIFIED before integration.
- **KR-CERT (KISA/KRCERT, kr-cert / boho.or.kr)**: no verifiable public API/feed found; search interface + email alerts only. UNVERIFIED for machine access.
- **NCSC UK**: no official public advisory API verified; advisories web/RSS-scrape only (third-party scrapers exist). SEARCH INTERFACE.
- **VulDB**: operational, strictly commercial API (see block 5).

#### Gaps
1. No verified machine-accessible Chinese (CNNVD is paywalled; CNVD no public API), Russian (FSB/NKTSKI vuln DBs are web-only), Indian (CERT-In RSS exists but was not verified this run — recommend follow-up), Korean (KR-CERT) or Arabic-national CERT feeds. Regional coverage achieved via JVN (JP), CERT-Bund (DE), CERT-FR (FR), NCSC-NL (NL), plus global aggregators.
2. CSAF adoption by national CERTs (NCSC-NL confirmed; BSI/CERT-FR/Red Hat also publish CSAF but unverified here) is a high-value follow-up track.
3. MISP TAXII-gated feeds (e.g., private CIRCL instances) and Shadowserver free reports API (daily network scan/botnet reports, keyless for own-ASN — not verified this run) are worth a follow-up mission.
4. Commercial-only gaps: VulDB pricing tiers beyond the two published; ANY.RUN TI Feeds quota numbers not published.

---

### STATUS NOTES (no/limited machine access — verified)
- **Radiopaedia** (Australia): NO official public API. Third-party wrappers exist (unofficial). Content CC-BY-NC-SA for articles but images restricted — treat as search interface; contact for licensing.
- **MSD Manuals (Merck Manuals)**: no public API; syndication/licensing only via commercial agreement.
- **NHS (UK) content**: NHS website content API was retired; syndication now via NHS.UK widgets/partnership — no open REST API verified in 2026.
- **GARD (NIH/NCATS)**: rarediseases.info.nih.gov — site operational, relaunched 2025; SEARCH INTERFACE only, no public API. Use Orphanet (#7 above) + RDAS (rdas.ncats.nih.gov, Neo4j browser, research-grade) instead.
- **jRCT (Japan)**: jrct.mhlw.go.jp — official registry, search interface only, no API; reach via ICTRP crawl (#5). Same for ChiCTR, CTRI (India), CRiS (Korea).
- **CIAP (Australia)**: NSW Health intranet clinical resource — no public access at all. Australian alternative: ADHA syndication Atom feed for SNOMED CT-AU/AMT releases (free clinician registration, digitalhealth.gov.au).
- **BioCaster (Japan, NII)**: research system, effectively dormant — do not rely on.

### GAPS & WARNINGS
1. **Migrations in flight (2026)**: PMC OA distribution (legacy FTP/OA API retired ~Aug 24, 2026 → AWS restructure) and WHO GHO OData endpoint deprecation — pin integration work after cutover or build adapters for both.
2. **No open symptom/diagnostic-knowledge API exists at authority level**: Infermedica (PL) and Isabel are commercial (tier D); OpenFDA `drug/event` + NLM ClinicalTables + MedlinePlus Connect are the closest free machine-readable substitutes.
3. **Imaging findings**: no free official API (Radiopaedia closed; RadLex via BioPortal only).
4. **Guidelines beyond UK**: WHO guidelines (IRIS repository — REST/OAI-PMH at iris.who.int, worth a future mission), USPSTF, and most national guideline bodies (US, DE, JP, IN) lack APIs.
5. **Surveillance paywall trend**: ProMED's 2025 shift to paid subscriptions breaks the historically free outbreak-feed ecosystem — budget or fall back to WHO EIOS (restricted) / RSS of national CDCs / GISAID.
6. **China/India/Japan medical literature**: CNKI (China) and Ichushi-Web (Japan) have no open APIs (commercial); J-STAGE (Japan) and IndMED/medIND (India) offer OAI-PMH/RSS — recommended as follow-up targets for regional coverage.

---

### GAPS & STATUS NOTES (searched, no machine access / not verified)
- **EUDAMED** (EU devices, Tier A): public site `ec.europa.eu/tools/eudamed` = SEARCH INTERFACE only; no public REST API verified. 4 modules (Actor, UDI/Device, NB-Certificates, Market Surveillance) MANDATORY since 28 May 2026 (Decision (EU) 2025/2371); DTX XML/XSD services exist for registered economic operators, not public read access. Vigilance module planned mandatory ~Q2 2027. Candidate `WARROOM_EUDAMED_URL` pending API publication.
- **Drug Interaction Knowledge Base (DIKB)**: academic/static, no maintained API found — superseded by RxNav Interaction API (free) / DrugBank DDI (paid).
- **PMDA Japan**: PMDA website + English "Reviews" pages = SEARCH INTERFACE; no public API verified. JADER (Japan ADR database) web-search only. India **CDSCO**: portal-only, no API. China **NMPA**: database search pages only. **MHRA (UK)**: Products website search; no official API. Recommend gaps list for future scraping-permission review.
- **WHO Prequalification of Medicines**: website + PDF/Excel listings; no API verified.
- **Withdrawn drugs**: no dedicated open API — best machine sources are EMA medicines dataset (withdrawn/refused status), DrugCentral `withdrawn` flags, ChEMBL `drug_warning` endpoint, FDA "Drug Safety Communications" RSS/FEED, and Discontinued Drug Product List in the FDA NDC/Drugs@FDA data.
- **EudraVigilance** public data: adrreports.eu = SEARCH INTERFACE (aggregate), no public API.
- **Pricing/access**: no global open drug-pricing API verified (MSH/HAI and national formularies are PDF/web); WHO MiND is web-only — flagged as gap.
- **Licensing caution summary**: PubChem/openFDA/NLM = public domain (freest); ChEMBL **CC BY-SA 3.0**, DrugCentral & PharmGKB **CC BY-SA 4.0** (share-alike propagates into derived KG distributions); GtoPdb share-alike (ODbL per third-party listing — confirm); DrugBank proprietary; VigiAccess © WHO/UMC restricted.

**Verification method (Domain 05):** all endpoints, rate limits, licenses and 2026 statuses confirmed via official docs (pubchem.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov, open.fda.gov, chembl.gitbook.io, EMA pages, EC EUDAMED overview, vaers.hhs.gov, health-products.canada.ca API guide, PharmGKB/ClinPGx policy pages, fda.gov Orange Book page) during the verification session.

## CATEGORY 08 — TRADITIONAL & INDIGENOUS MEDICINE

**Sources in this category: 21**

All sources from the Domain 06 report (verified as of 2026 via direct HTTP probing of every endpoint (curl), browser rendering where bot-blocked (Kew), official docs/news. Status codes cited below are from live probes of that session).

**Evidence-classification legend (mission-critical — NEVER strip these flags):** Each block flags its evidence scheme: `TRAD` = recorded traditional use / pharmacopoeia record (NOT clinical proof); `EXP` = experimental/literature bioactivity; `PRED` = computational prediction (e.g., target inference); `NOMEN` = nomenclatural only; `CLIN` = clinical. War Room must never flatten TRAD/PRED records into clinical evidence.

GetApiVersion/ItemSearch). Free key, signup: https://www.biodiversitylibrary.org/getapikey.aspx (official; 403 to curl = bot protection). JSON/XML; IIIF image service on many items; Internet Archive bulk. License: mixed PD/CC. `WARROOM_BHL_API_KEY`. EASY. Historical herbals = TRAD-historical evidence.

**20. Wellcome Collection** — Tier B; digitized herbals/medical manuscripts (incl. Arabic, Indian, Chinese medical MSS). Catalogue REST API `https://api.wellcomecollection.org/catalogue/v2/works` (probe 200, no key, JSON) + **IIIF Presentation/Image** (`iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/{b-number}`). CC BY/PD per work. `WARROOM_WELLCOME_API`. EASY.

**21. KNApSAcK Family** — Tier B, NAIST Japan; species–metabolite DB incl. Asian medicinal plants; https://www.knapsackfamily.com/KNApSAcK/ (probe 200). Search interface + downloadable data; free academic. (Dedicated REST page 404'd — treat as SEARCH INTERFACE + bulk.) TRAD/EXP per record.

---

### Dead / deprecated — do not wire
- `old.tcmsp-e.com` — dead (000); use `tcmsp-e.com`.
- `megabionet.org/tcmid` — 302→bare IP, unmaintained; use `bidd.group/TCMID`.
- `bioinfo.org/symmap` mirror — dead (000); use `symmap.org`.
- `prota.org` — dead (000); use `prota4u.org`.
- `lotus.naturalproducts.net` — officially unmaintained/phasing out; use Wikidata SPARQL.
- ChEBI SOAP web services — retired Oct 2025; new REST only.
- TCMBanK (tcm.cmu.edu.tw), CPMCP — reported inaccessible in 2025 literature.

### Gaps
1. **No open machine-readable API exists for codified Ayurvedic formulations** — TKDL is NDA-gated; IMPPAT is the open surrogate.
2. **Amazonian / indigenous South American medicine**: no verified structured database with API; records live in ethnopharmacology journals (→ suggest PubMed/Europe PMC + SciELO ingestion).
3. **African traditional-use records** beyond PROTA/SANCDB (e.g., PRELUDE, national pharmacopoeias) are PDF/search-only — no APIs found.
4. **Russian/Central Asian & Arabic/Unani (Greco-Arab) digitized corpora** exist only as manuscripts (Wellcome/BHL IIIF) — no structured APIs.
5. PFAF/UTP/NAEB/SymMap/TCMSP/ETCM are single-host fragile academic sites — recommend immediate bulk mirroring where licenses permit; NAEB's broken TLS is a warning sign.

### SUPPLEMENTARY / STATUS NOTES (verified, not full blocks)

- **GEO / SRA / ClinVar / dbSNP** — no separate APIs; all machine access is via NCBI E-utilities (#1: `db=gds` for GEO, `db=sra`, `db=clinvar`, `db=snp`) plus NCBI FTP bulk. SRA raw reads: SRA Toolkit / NCBI cloud (AWS/GCP open-data buckets). Do NOT build separate connectors.
- **Expression Atlas (EMBL-EBI, incl. Single Cell Expression Atlas)** — programmatic access exists via JSON endpoints under https://www.ebi.ac.uk/gxa/json/... and FTP bulk (ftp.ebi.ac.uk/pub/databases/microarray/data/atlas/), but public API documentation is sparse/legacy (old /gxa/api retired). Classify as REST (lightly documented) + BULK. Tier A. Difficulty MODERATE. Verify endpoints before integration.
- **HMDB (Human Metabolome Database, Canada)** — ⚠ NO public REST API. Downloads at hmdb.ca/downloads (XML/SDF/FASTA/CSV) free for academic use; commercial use requires permission; API access only by contacting the team. Classify as BULK DOWNLOAD (registration/license-gated). Tier B. Do not promise an HMDB API connector.
- **DepMap (Broad Institute)** — ⚠ No stable documented public REST API (portal /api/ paths undocumented; may 404). Supported programmatic path: release matrices on Figshare via Figshare API (https://api.figshare.com/v2/articles/{id}) + https://depmap.org/portal/api/download/files table endpoint for current-release URLs. Classify as BULK DOWNLOAD via third-party REPOSITORY API. Tier A data. Env: `WARROOM_DEPMAP_FIGSHARE_ARTICLE_ID`.
- **ProteomeXchange announcement feed** — RSS: http://groups.google.com/group/proteomexchange/feed/rss_v2_0_msgs.xml + ProteomeCentral PX XML per dataset (http://proteomecentral.proteomexchange.org). Useful as FEED connector for new proteomics datasets.
- **QuickGO** (www.ebi.ac.uk/QuickGO/api) — complementary REST API specifically for GO *annotations* (gene–term associations), which OLS4 does not serve. Recommend as add-on to source #13 (cross-referenced in Category 05).

### GAPS & CAVEATS
1. **KEGG legal gap**: No free bulk access; budget for academic FTP subscription or substitute Reactome/WikiPathways for open pipeline use. Commercial War Room use requires Pathway Solutions license.
2. **No high-quality open CRISPR-perturbation REST API**: DepMap is download-only; BioGRID ORCS REST covers literature screens (key required). LINCS CLUE API (clue.io, free academic key) exists for perturbational signatures but was out of scope to fully verify — flag for a future mission.
3. **Regional coverage**: INSDC triad covered (NCBI/ENA/DDBJ). No equivalent open APIs found for China (NGDC/CNCB — BIG Data Center offers genome databases with some APIs; NGDC GSA largely mirrors INSDC but API documentation is thin/Chinese-language — candidate for a dedicated Asia mission), Russia (no major open molbio API), Africa/Latin America (rely on INSDC mirrors; H3Africa data is largely controlled-access via EGA).
4. **Rate-limit transparency**: Only NCBI, Ensembl, ENA publish hard limits; most others are "fair use" — implement client-side throttling (≤2–10 req/s) and respect Retry-After.
5. **Migration watch items for 2026**: Ensembl platform migration (beta.ensembl.org) may alter REST surface; OLS3→OLS4 completed but old `/ols/api` URLs should be updated to `/ols4/api`; NCBI Datasets v1 deprecated.

**LIVE-PROBE RESULTS SUMMARY (Domain 07 verification session):** GBIF 200 ✓, OBIS v3 200 ✓, GloBI 200 ✓, BacDive v2 200 ✓ (no auth!), EOL API 200 ✓ (alive, real JSON returned), WoRMS REST 200 ✓, ITIS JSON 200 ✓, NCBI E-utils 200 ✓, ICTV downloads 200 ✓, eBird keygen 200 ✓, Index Fungorum 200 ✓, BirdLife DataZone 200 ✓, Global Ecosystems 200 ✓. ---

### STATUS NOTES ON OTHER MISSION ITEMS
- **Extinct organisms**: covered via IUCN v4 (EX/EW categories), PBDB (`extant=false`, deep time), BirdLife (Possibly Extinct flags). No standalone extinct-species API exists.
- **Ecosystems/biomes**: IUCN Global Ecosystem Typology (https://global-ecosystems.org, 200 ✓) offers profile pages + GIS/CSV downloads (no REST API); WWF Terrestrial Ecoregions via GIS downloads. Flagged as MODERATE bulk-only sources — recommend a future dedicated probe.
- **CABI Invasive Species Compendium**: rich dossiers but gated/commercial, no public API — use GRIIS instead.
- **Protists**: beyond BacDive/LPSN (prokaryotes), eukaryotic protists covered by WoRMS (AlgaeBase-linked) and IRMNG (same Aphia REST platform — `https://www.irmng.org/rest/...`), live in 2026.

### GAPS NOTE
No public machine API for: BirdLife (request-gated), POWO (unofficial/bot-blocked), Index Fungorum (legacy SOAP only), TRY (manual request cycle), BGCI tools (CSV only), ICTV (spreadsheet only). Ecosystem/biome data lacks a unified REST API globally. Regional/global coverage is heavily Europe/US-centric for infrastructure; Asian nodes exist mainly as GBIF national nodes rather than standalone APIs (China's CVH/NSII, Japan's J-IBIS/S-Net, India's IBP are web portals without documented public REST APIs — verification recommended as a follow-up mission). Rate limits are unpublished for most open biodiversity APIs; plan conservative pacing (≤1 req/s) except NCBI (3–10/s) and iNaturalist (60/min).
# EARTH KNOWLEDGE SOURCE REGISTRY — PART 3 (Categories 11–17)

Reorganized from verified wave-2 source reports (files 09–13). Pure reorganization — no sources added, re-verified, or altered; all 34-field blocks verbatim from their source reports (field order per source file's own schema line; file 12 blocks carry field 1 (name) in the bold title line).

Source files: 09_history_archaeology.md · 10_government_law_economics.md · 11_science_engineering_patents.md · 12_maps_space.md · 13_academic_archives_museums.md.

---

## CATEGORY 11 — EARTH & ENVIRONMENTAL

**Sources in this category: 11**

Weather/climate APIs (file 12, GROUP C, sources 14–22) plus elevation/bathymetry (file 12, GROUP D sources 23–24). Space weather (NOAA SWPC, file 12 source 30) stays with Category 19 — not included here. Base maps, satellite/earth-observation and national mapping sources from file 12 remain in Category 18.

**Cross-reference:** NASA Earthdata CMR and Copernicus CDSE (file 12, sources 10/8) carry climate/earth-observation content but are registered in Category 18 (Maps, GIS & Satellite).

**Cross-reference:** NOAA Space Weather Prediction Center (SWPC) is registered in Category 19 (Space & Astronomy).

**Cross-reference:** PANGAEA paleoenvironmental data is registered in Category 12 (Human Origins & Archaeology) as human-origins context.

---

### Category notes

From file 12 "STATUS NOTES (2026)" — weather/climate-relevant items:

- Met Office DataPoint: retired 1 Dec 2025. No like-for-like replacement; Weather DataHub covers site forecasts/obs + map images; regional text forecasts & daily extremes now web-only (GAP).
- JMA XML PUSH distribution ended 2020; several legacy warning telegram codes end ~2028 (migration to VPWW55–61 series).
- ECMWF legacy CDS decommissioned Sep 2024; cds-beta URL also dead — must use cds.climate.copernicus.eu/api + cdsapi ≥0.7.7.

From file 12 "TOP GAPS" — weather/climate-relevant items:

- China: FengYun meteorological data (data.nsmc.org.cn) portal-only, registration required, no documented API.
- India: MOSDAC (Indian meteorological sat) — portal-only.
- Met Office daily extremes / regional text forecasts lost API access entirely with DataPoint retirement.
- Real-time seismic/feeds were out of scope of file 12 (noted there as likely belonging to this registry category).

**Environmental-data blind spot (mission-level gap):** no UNEP, US EPA, or European Environment Agency (EEA) machine APIs were verified in this mission's wave-2 reports — air/water quality, emissions, and environmental-indicator data currently have no verified dedicated environmental-agency API in this registry; nearest partial coverage is Open-Meteo air-quality model data, ECMWF CAMS (ADS), and Met.no LUFT endpoints above. Priority follow-up category for the next wave.

## CATEGORY 12 — HUMAN ORIGINS & ARCHAEOLOGY

**Sources in this category: 15**

Archaeology / human-origins / ancient-world sources from file 09 (GROUP A sources A1–A8, GROUP B sources B1–B3, GROUP C sources C1–C2, GROUP E sources E1–E2). World-history text corpora and primary-source archives from file 09 are in Category 13; historical-newspaper sources (impresso, NLI, ANNO) and all newspaper status/discontinued notes are deferred to Category 22.

**Cross-reference:** World Historical Gazetteer (A8, full block below) is a historical-places gazetteer — cross-ref to Category 13 (World History) and Category 18 (Maps/GIS gazetteers).

**Cross-reference:** Paleobiology Database (fossil record incl. hominins) is registered in wave1/07_biology_biodiversity.md — closest machine source for hominin fossils.

**Cross-reference:** Pelagios/Recogito ecosystem data flows into WHG (see notes) — register WHG, not Recogito, for data.

**Cross-reference:** CHGIS and CBDB (China historical) registered in wave2/16_regional_specialized.md; Chronicling America, p3k14c, IntChron registered per mission brief.

### Category notes

From file 09 "STATUS NOTES — VERIFIED NO OPEN MACHINE ACCESS / COMMERCIAL / DEGRADED" (archaeology/human-origins relevant):

- **Portable Antiquities Scheme (finds.org.uk, UK)** — Tier A content. NO documented public API found; `format=json` URLs return 403 (Cloudflare) to non-browser clients. No official open-data dump verified. Treat as SEARCH INTERFACE. (Potential partner route: British Museum data requests.)
- **Trismegistos (KU Leuven)** — Tier B. SEARCH INTERFACE; no public API documented (TM numbers are cross-linked by EDH/papyri.info, which partially mitigates).
- **eHRAF World Cultures / eHRAF Archaeology (HRAF, Yale)** — Tier D (membership nonprofit). CONFIRMED via official FAQ: subscription, IP/OpenAthens auth, NO API. Only global paragraph-indexed ethnographic/archaeological full text — license required.
- **Database of Religious History (DRH, UBC/LSE)** — Tier B. SEARCH INTERFACE (religiondatabase.org); no public API found. Partial machine route: entry PDFs archived with DOIs (CC BY 4.0) in UBC cIRcle DSpace — harvestable via DSpace OAI-PMH, but not the quantitative data.
- **Pelagios Network / Recogito / Peripleo** — Tier B/C ecosystem. Recogito is an annotation WORKSPACE, not a read API; machine data flows into WHG (A8) as Linked Places. Register WHG, not Recogito, for data.

From file 09 "DISCONTINUED / MIGRATED":

- **dev.tdar.org (tDAR developer site)** — DEAD (connection refused). Replacement: tdar-arch.atlassian.net Confluence.

From file 09 "GAPS" (archaeology/human-origins relevant):

7. **Paleoanthropology-specific**: no dedicated hominin fossil/occurrence API found (NESPOS search interface; Smithsonian Human Origins no API). PBDB (registered) is the closest; radiocarbon via IntChron/p3k14c (registered). Gap for a curated hominin-sites layer.
8. **National archaeological site registers** (UK HERs, Canmore, Heritage Gateway, national monuments services): mostly no public APIs; ARIADNE (A3) is the aggregation workaround.

## CATEGORY 13 — WORLD HISTORY & PRIMARY SOURCES

**Sources in this category: 13**

World-history databanks, historical GIS, and premodern/primary-source text corpora from file 09 (GROUP A sources A9, A10, A13–A15; GROUP D sources D1–D2; GROUP E sources E3–E8). e-periodica (D2) included — its own block notes it covers journals/periodicals, not newspapers. Newspaper/news-archive sources from file 09 (impresso A11, NLI A12, ANNO D3) and all newspaper status/discontinued notes (Google News Archive, The European Library, World Digital Library, HTRC, commercial newspaper databases, Latin America/South Asia/China/Russia/Arabic-press gaps) are deferred to Category 22.

**Cross-reference:** Seshat's spatial arm Cliopatria and D-PLACE are registered in Category 12 (full blocks there).

**Cross-reference:** WHG (World Historical Gazetteer) full block is in Category 12 — the gazetteer backbone these history sources link into.

**Cross-reference:** OpenHistoricalMap (below) is OSM-stack infrastructure — cross-ref to Category 18 (Maps/GIS) for tile/Overpass usage.

**Cross-reference:** EHRI (below) is archival-description infrastructure — cross-ref to Category 21 (Archives, Libraries & Museums).

**Cross-reference:** QDL and e-periodica deliver content via IIIF — same IIIF stack family as the museum/library sources in Category 21.

### Category notes

From file 09 "STATUS NOTES" (world-history/primary-source relevant):

- **TLG (Thesaurus Linguae Graecae, UC Irvine)** — Tier D/B. Subscription; no open API (open-access subset only via web).
- **Seshat note**: license CC BY-NC-SA — fine for research KB, blocked for commercial War Room deployments.

From file 09 "GAPS" (world-history relevant):

9. **World-history event/timeline datasets**: beyond Seshat/Cliopatria and Wikidata, no open machine-readable global event/timeline infrastructure verified (GeaCron commercial w/o open API; Running Reality unverified).
10. **Old Bailey Online / Founders Online / Documenting the American South**: US primary-source sites with reported downloads — unverified this session; candidate follow-ups.

**Deferred to Category 22 notes** (per split): Google News Archive scanning program (DISCONTINUED 2011), The European Library (shut 2019), World Digital Library (migrated to loc.gov 2021), HTRC tools (sunset end-2026), HNDM/Hemeroteca Digital Brasileira/Memoria Chilena/Digital Pasifik/NDLI/DDB Zeitungsportal/e-newspaperarchives.ch/dLOC/British Newspaper Archive/Newspapers.com/NewspaperArchive/GenealogyBank/Readex/ProQuest/East View status entries, and newspaper GAPS 1–6.

## CATEGORY 14 — GOVERNMENT & LAW

**Sources in this category: 32**

All government/law sources from file 10: legislation/parliaments/gazettes/courts (sources 1–10), hybrid & no-API legal (11–16), sanctions + Interpol + EU BRIS (17–21, 26–27), budgets/spending/procurement (28–34), elections (35–36). Company registers/identifiers (GLEIF, OpenCorporates, Companies House, SEC EDGAR) and central banks/economic statistics are in Category 15.

**Cross-reference:** GLEIF/OpenCorporates/Companies House/SEC EDGAR company-identifier blocks are in Category 15; EU BRIS (no-API status entry) kept here with the other legal-status entries.

**Cross-reference:** EU TED + OCDS + FTS + USAspending overlap on procurement/spending — see their field-34 cross-links.

**Cross-reference:** UN Comtrade, IMF, World Bank, OECD SDMX, V-Dem registered elsewhere (per file 10 header cross-ref).

**Cross-reference:** CURIA/CJEU case law has no API; partial mitigation via EUR-Lex/CELLAR RDF (source 1 above).

### Category notes

From file 10 "STATUS NOTES — NO/OFFICIAL MACHINE ACCESS ABSENT":

- India eGazette (#16): no official API; unofficial paid wrappers only. GAP.
- EU BRIS (#26): explicitly no API; existence-check UI only. GAP.
- UN Treaty Collection (#13): no API; per-treaty XLS only. GAP.
- ILO NATLEX/NORMLEX (#14) & FAOLEX (#15): search interfaces; bot protection; no bulk.
- Germany Gesetze im Internet (#11): verified NO query API — but complete authoritative XML corpus via gii-toc.xml (effectively solves bulk need).
- Interpol ws-public (#27): live but Akamai-blocked for scripts; use OpenSanctions interpol dataset as pragmatic path.
- OpenSpending (#34): API dead; only Fiscal Data Package spec survives.
- Elections generally: only ElectionGuide (token) + IDEA (Excel) verified; no machine-readable results feeds from most national EMBs (Brazil TSE, India ECI are portals/downloads; US FEC API exists but out of this wave's depth — candidate for next wave: fec.gov API, open key at api.data.gov).

From file 10 "GAPS & FOLLOW-UPS" (government/law relevant):

1. China: no verified open machine access for national law (国家法律法规数据库 flk.npc.gov.cn is search-only) or company register (GSXT captcha-walled). High-priority gap.
2. Russia: pravo.gov.ru / Consultant are UI-only; sanctions-adjacent registers (FSB/Minjust) no API. Gap.
3. Middle East: no open legal/gazette APIs found (Saudi uqn.gov.sa, UAE legislation portal are search interfaces).
4. Africa: intentionally skipped per mission (prior registry) — note AU/national gazettes largely scan-only.
5. Parliaments beyond US: UK Parliament API (members-api.parliament.uk) and EU Parliament (data.europarl.europa.eu) verified-worthy candidates for next wave; Brazil Câmara/Senado have open APIs (dadosabertos.camara.leg.br) — strong candidate.
7. Court data outside US: no equivalents to CourtListener at scale; EU CURIA (CJEU) has no API (CELLAR RDF covers case law — partial mitigation via EUR-Lex).
9. Elections results microdata: no global machine standard; IPU Parline API candidate for parliaments data.

File 10 verification log (2026-08-08, verbatim, covers Categories 14+15): live HTTP probes — eurlex sparql 200, federalregister 200, govinfo(DEMO) 200, congress.gov 403(key), legislation.gov.uk 202, japan egov 200, au odata 200, gazette 202, courtlistener 200, canada xml 200, gii-toc 200, untc 200, natlex 301→403(bot), faolex 403(bot), ofac sdn 302→S3 200, eu fsd rss live, un sc xml 302→200, uk ofsi page 200, opensanctions api 401(key), gleif 200, opencorporates 401(key), companies house 401(key), edgar 200, usaspending 200, ted 405(GET→POST ok), chilecompra 203(ticket), fts 200/301, ilostat 200, fred 400(badkey), ecb 200, boc 200, bis 200, estat 200(errpayload), openspending api: connection failed (dead).

**Scopus (Elsevier) — OPERATIONAL, PAID**
api.elsevier.com/content/search/scopus; institutional subscription + API key (Elsevier Developer Portal, dev.elsevier.com); free limited "Scopus API" for subscribers; weekly updates; Tier D; MODERATE. Verify institution license before integration. No change 2026.

**Web of Science (Clarivate) — OPERATIONAL, PAID**
Clarivate Developer Portal (developer.clarivate.com); WoS API Expanded/Lite; institutional subscription required; Tier D; MODERATE. Note: RSCI removed from WoS May 2022 (Clarivate exited Russia).

**Lens.org Scholarly API — OPERATIONAL, FREEMIUM/PAID**
api.lens.org; token-based; paid plans for scholarly/patent API (institutional & commercial tiers; limited free academic use by application); Tier D; MODERATE. Cross-ref: overlaps OpenAlex/S2 which are free.

**CNKI (China) — NO OPEN API**
cnki.net; commercial database; no public/open API verified 2026; access via institutional subscription; some Chinese university proxy integrations only. Tier D. HARD. **Gap: no machine channel to Chinese national scholarship at scale** (partial alternatives: OpenAlex CNKI-derived coverage, Crossref DOIs, NSSD 国家哲学社会科学文献中心 — UI only).

**DBpia / RISS (Korea) — NO OPEN API**
dbpia.co.kr; commercial; no public API verified. RISS (riss.kr, KERIS) — national theses; UI only, no open API verified. Tier D/A resp. HARD.

**Airiti Library (Taiwan) — NO OPEN API**
airitilibrary.com; commercial; no public API verified 2026. Tier D. HARD. (Alternative for Taiwan: NCL Taiwan 台灣博碩士論文系統 — UI only.)

**eLibrary.ru / RSCI — PAID API ONLY**
eLIBRARY.RU API exists but commercial/paid only (confirmed via dissertation documentation 2025); no free academic access; RSCI removed from Web of Science May 2022, continues on eLibrary platform; UI registration required; Russian-language only. Tier D. HARD. Open alternative for Russian OA: CyberLeninka (A11).

**SSRN (Elsevier) — NO PUBLIC API**
ssrn.com; acquired by Elsevier 2016; no public API; RSS feeds per series; scraping against ToS; preprints partly mirrored in OpenAlex/Crossref. Tier D. HARD.

**Paperity — NO OPEN API (custom extracts)**
paperity.org; 11M+ OA full-text papers; no self-serve API; customized database extracts by email (services@paperity.org; limited subsets free for academic use); RSS feeds on searches. Tier C. MODERATE (email negotiation).

**Latindex — SEARCH INTERFACE ONLY**
latindex.org (UNAM); regional journal directory for Ibero-America; no API; UI + some CSV exports per country node. Tier B. Use Redalyc/SciELO APIs for the content layer.

From file 13 "GAPS" (scholarly-infrastructure items):

1. **China**: CNKI (E4), National Library of China — no open API. Best proxies: OpenAlex/Crossref coverage; wanfang/ NSSD UI-only.
2. **Korea**: DBpia/RISS/NLK — no open APIs; KISTI path Korean-language gated (A14).
3. **Taiwan**: Airiti, NCL thesis system — no APIs.
4. **Russia**: eLibrary paid-only API; RSL no API. Open island: CyberLeninka OAI-PMH.
5. (journal layer) Latin America: only SciELO/Redalyc journal-layer APIs and LA Referencia (OAI-PMH aggregator, recommend registering separately) — national-library items deferred to Category 21.

From file 11 "STATUS NOTES (2026)" (science-archive items):

- **DEGRADED/TRANSITION**: Legacy cds.cern.ch superseded by repository.cern (InvenioRDM). api.oeis.org SSL-degraded (probe: HTTP 525) — oeis.org/search?fmt=json works.

### Category notes

From file 11 "STATUS NOTES (2026)" (patent/IP items):

- **DISCONTINUED**: USPTO PEDS (retired 2025-03-14) and BDSS → replaced by **USPTO Open Data Portal** (api key via MyUSPTO + ID.me). Old ped.uspto.gov / pairbulk dead.
- **DEGRADED/TRANSITION**: PatentsView API/search paused 2026-03-20 during migration into ODP; downloads via ODP still live.
- **CONFIRMED NO-API (search interface only)**: WIPO PATENTSCOPE (ToS bans automation; paid SFTP/SOAP only), J-PlatPat, CNIPA, **INPI Brazil live register** (RPI weekly XML + dados.gov.br annual biblio are the clean bulk paths).
- **PAYWALLED APIs**: Lens (14-day free trial then paid), WIPO PCT data (CHF 400+/yr).

From file 11 "TOP GAPS" (patent/IP items):

1. **Japan & China patent legal-status machine access** — no official API from JPO/CNIPA; only partial substitutes (EPO OPS INPADOC legal events, Google Patents).
3. **India (CGPDTM/IPO)** and most African/LatAm IP offices — no verified public APIs found; INPI Brazil has good weekly RPI XML bulk but no query API. India patent office e-services portal has no documented public API.
5. **CrossRef-quality citation data for patents** — Lens paid; PatentsView in migration; EPO OPS citations endpoint has weekly quota.

### Category notes

DISCONTINUED / DEGRADED (from wave-2 file 12 status notes):
- Copernicus Data Space: OpenSearch (resto) Catalogue API decommissioned 2 Feb 2026; legacy STAC endpoint catalogue.dataspace.copernicus.eu/stac deprecated 17 Nov 2025 → new https://stac.dataspace.copernicus.eu/v1/. (Old SciHub closed Oct 2023.)
- JAXA G-Portal: FTP disabled (SFTP only); service relaunched 1 Apr 2025 after extended 2024 outage.

TOP GAPS (maps/GIS/EO, from wave-2 file 12):
1. China (CNSA/CLEP): Lunar & Planetary Data Release System (moon.bao.ac.cn / clpds.bao.ac.cn) is SEARCH INTERFACE + download only — no documented API; needs account, Chinese-language UI. FengYun meteorological data (data.nsmc.org.cn) similar: portal-only, registration required.
2. Russia: Roscosmos/RosHydromet EO & weather — no open machine APIs found; GLONASS precise orbits via IAC FTP (semi-public). KRAS/Russian gazetteer gaps.
3. Africa: no operational African national mapping/EO API identified (African Regional Data Cube = web sandbox); SANSA EO portal limited. Rely on OSM/Overture/Copernicus.
4. Middle East: UAE/KSA national geoportal APIs not verified; Saudi NSG portal is UI-only so far.
5. Sentinel Asia (JAXA) — data access is web-UI/request-based for emergency members only; no public API.
6. India: Bhuvan WMS layer inventory undocumented; no official STAC. MOSDAC (Indian meteorological sat) — portal-only.
7. Mapillary rate limits unpublished; LINZ/Kadaster/GA verified at documentation level only (endpoints listed, not probed).

Cross-ref: weather/climate APIs (Open-Meteo, ECMWF CDS, NOAA NCEI/NWS, Met.no, DWD, Met Office, JMA XML, OpenWeatherMap) are registered in the weather/climate category (from same wave-2 file 12).

### Category notes

DISCONTINUED / DEGRADED (from wave-2 file 12 status notes):
- CelesTrak: 5-digit NORAD catalog exhaustion ~Jul 2026 — TLE format unavailable for catalog numbers ≥100000; adopt OMM (XML/KVN/JSON/CSV) parsing now.
- Space-Track: legacy tle/tle_latest/omm request classes deprecated → use `gp` class.
- NASA Exoplanet Archive: legacy nstedAPI being phased out → TAP.

TOP GAPS (space/astronomy, from wave-2 file 12):
1. ZTF/LSST transient alert streams: machine access exists via brokers (ANTARES, AMPEL, Fink — Kafka streams, registration) — worth a dedicated follow-up verification pass (not fully verified this session).
2. IRSA & HEASARC (NASA/IPAC + HEASARC Browse) — both have TAP/REST interfaces (irsa.ipac.caltech.edu/TAP; HEASARC xamin TAP) verified only via astroquery references this session, not primary docs — candidate additions for wave 3.
3. ESO Archive & ESA Science Archive (ESASky) — programmatic TAP/SIA2 exist; not verified this session.
4. China (CNSA/CLEP) Lunar & Planetary Data Release System — SEARCH INTERFACE only (see Category 18 notes).

### 4. ISO / IEC / ASTM / DIN / JIS / GB standards — STATUS NOTE (no APIs)
**ISO** (iso.org): no public API; OBP (Online Browsing Platform) is a search interface; standards are PAID PDFs. **IEC** (webstore.iec.ch): paid, no public API. **ASTM, DIN (Beuth), JIS (JISC webdesk), GB standards (China, std.samr.gov.cn / openstd)**: paid or restricted portals; China's GB **mandatory national standards full text is free to read** at openstd.samr.gov.cn (HTML images, no API). **None offer machine APIs** — War Room must treat as SEARCH INTERFACE / licensed-content category; discovery metadata only. (Verified via official portals; no official API documentation exists for any of them.)

### Category notes

From wave-2 file 11 STATUS NOTES (2026) and TOP GAPS (engineering/standards/materials-relevant):
- **CONFIRMED NO-API (search interface only)**: NIST Chemistry WebBook, NIMS MatNavi (scraping explicitly banned), ISO/IEC/ASTM/DIN/JIS/GB stores.
- **PAYWALLED APIs**: CCDC CSD (site license, no redistribution), MPDS (freemium), IEEE full text (subscription).
- **Standards full text gap** — only ETSI/ITU free; ISO/IEC/ASTM/GB have no APIs and paid/restricted text. IEEE standards metadata available but text paywalled.
- **Chemistry computed/experimental properties beyond PubChem/ChEMBL** — NIST WebBook/TRC paywall-or-scrape situation persists; IUPAC offers no data API.
- **NIMS experimental materials data legally unreachable in bulk** (from TOP GAPS item 1).

Cross-ref: science archives (arXiv, INSPIRE, ADS, CERN, HEPData, zbMATH, OEIS, PDG) and all patent/IP sources from wave-2 file 11 are registered in Categories 16 and 17.

### Category notes

From wave-2 file 13 STATUS NOTES 2026 (discontinued / degraded / migrations — archives/libraries/museums items):
2. **Trove API v2 DISCONTINUED Sept 2024** → v3 (`api.trove.nla.gov.au/v3`, X-API-KEY header). May 2025: NLA revoked some long-standing researcher keys without notice — key-continuity risk. [B1]
3. **NARA legacy Catalog + API v1 RETIRED Sept 2023** → Catalog API v2 (key via Catalog_API@nara.gov; 10k queries/month default). [C1]
4. **Artstor RETIRED 2024-08-01** → merged into JSTOR. **JSTOR Constellate DISCONTINUED 2025-07-01**; JSTOR Text Analyzer retired 2024-08-30; no public API — TDM by dataset agreement only. [D14]
6. **RecordSearch (NAA) DEGRADED**: no official API; Anzacs API discontinued; Cloudflare bot protection broke community harvesting (May 2025). [C5]
7. **Polona API DEGRADED**: legacy documented API paths 404 (verified 2026-08); current API undocumented. [B13]
9. **DigitalNZ API key now OPTIONAL** (unauthenticated public access with shared rate cap). [B2]
11. **Libris legacy endpoints transitional**: Libris XL JSON-LD REST /find + SPARQL + EMM dumps are the supported path (docs at libris.kb.se/api/docs); site behind Anubis anti-bot. [B7]

From wave-2 file 13 GAPS (archives/libraries/museums items):
5. **Latin American national libraries** (BN Brasil, BN México, BN Argentina): no public APIs — only SciELO/Redalyc journal-layer APIs and LA Referencia (OAI-PMH aggregator, recommend registering separately).
6. **Middle East/Türkiye**: Millî Kütüphane no API; no Arabic-national-library machine channel found in this wave.
7. **National archives of France, Australia, Japan (JACAR)**: no public APIs (France partial data.gouv CSVs). NARA/TNA remain the API-accessible anchors; Germany via DDB.
8. **Museums without APIs**: Tokyo National Museum (use ColBase via Japan Search), e-Museum, ArtUK (aggressive bot protection), RMN-Grand Palais, Google Arts & Culture.
9. **Hungaricana (HU), NKP Czech OAI, BNP Portugal OAI**: historical OAI-PMH endpoints unverified/unreachable from this sandbox — verify from EU-network vantage before registering.
10. **Unauthenticated probing limits**: several gov domains (KB Sweden Anubis, Finna/NL Cloudflare, NARA Cloudflare) block naive bots — War Room connectors need proper UA + documented endpoints + keys.

Cross-ref: scholarly research infrastructure (Group A aggregators/citation APIs, KISTI) and commercial scholarly status checks (E1–E10, E15) from wave-2 file 13 are registered in the academic-research category by another integration pass. Japan Search is also cross-referenced from Category 25 (regional/specialized).

### Category notes

DISCONTINUED / MIGRATED (from wave-2 file 09, with official replacements):
- **Google News Archive scanning program** — DISCONTINUED 2011 (Google statement via Search Engine Land, 20 May 2011). ~2,000 scanned titles remain browsable; NO API ever existed; search degraded (no pre-1970 date filtering). Replacement: national newspaper APIs (Trove/Delpher/Gallica/impresso — registered or above) + Internet Archive collections.
- **The European Library (TEL)** — DISCONTINUED: aggregation frozen 31 Dec 2016; portal permanently shut 2019 (official Europeana/TEL page). Replacement: Europeana (registered).
- **World Digital Library (UNESCO/LOC)** — DISCONTINUED 2020 charter end; 2021 content migrated to loc.gov collections (official LOC page). Replacement: LOC JSON API + original institutions.
- **HTRC (HathiTrust Research Center) tools** — SUNSET end-2026 (cross-ref Africa registry; JSTOR TDM support as partial successor).

NEWSPAPER-ARCHIVE GAPS (from wave-2 file 09):
1. **Latin America**: no machine-accessible national newspaper archive found (HNDM no API; BN Brazil no API; Memoria Chilena no API; BDPI Ibero-American OAI aggregator appears defunct — unverified). dLOC OAI-PMH post-rebuild unconfirmed. Highest-priority gap for the mission.
2. **South Asia**: no national newspaper/book API (NDLI undocumented; Digital Library of India fragmented; no Indian newspaper-archive API). GRETIL/SARIT cover premodern texts only.

Cross-refs:
- Europeana Newspapers APIs (newspaper aggregation, IIIF/OAI) — registered in the Europeana/general reference registry.
- Qatar Digital Library (IIIF) is manuscripts/Gulf records, not news — registered in Category 25 (regional/specialized, file 16 #41); also flagged in wave-2 file 09 (D1).
- National newspaper archives also reachable via library APIs in Category 21 (Trove B1, KB/Delpher B10, DigitalNZ/Papers Past).

### Category notes

STATUS NOTES (discontinued / degraded / replaced — from wave-2 file 14):
- OECD: **old api.stats.oecd.org SDMX-JSON DEPRECATED** → new SDMX API at sdmx.oecd.org/public/rest (v1 stable; v2 partial). Verified via official docs.
- UNESCO UIS: **SDMX API EOL since June 2020**; **UIS.Stat browser retired Feb 2025** → new Data API (apiportal.uis.unesco.org) + BDDS bulk.
- Stats NZ: **NZ.Stat disconnected 2024-09-13** → Aotearoa Data Explorer; **Infoshare retirement scheduled** (migration from late 2026, completion ~2027).
- ABS: **ABS.Stat retired 2023** → ABS Data API (data.api.abs.gov.au, .Stat Suite).
- Istat: legacy sdmx.istat.it redirects → esploradati.istat.it/SDMXWS (verified).
- CBS Netherlands: new OData v4 catalog at datasets.cbs.nl (verified) alongside classic opendata.cbs.nl v3 + feed.
- SCB Sweden: new PxWeb API v2 at statistikdatabasen.scb.se/api/v2 alongside classic v1.
- Rosstat/EMISS: DEGRADED for non-Russian IPs (403 WAF observed); works domestically.
- China NBS: no official API; easyquery.htm WAF-blocks datacenter IPs (403 observed); some series suspended since 2023.
- TurkStat, ITU, UNIDO, WIPO IPstats, DGBAS, NSO Thailand, GSO Vietnam: no official public API (search interface + bulk only). [ITU/UNIDO/WIPO blocks are in Category 24]
- Sandbox note: api.census.gov, inegi.org.mx, api.scb.se, nzdotstat.stats.govt.nz, gadm.org, fedstat.ru, gso.gov.vn were unreachable from the hunting sandbox (geo/WAF), corroborated via official docs/maintained clients instead of live probes.

GAPS (countries/regions lacking verified machine APIs as of 2026 — from wave-2 file 14):
- Asia: Pakistan PBS, Bangladesh BBS, Sri Lanka DCS, Nepal CBS, Iran SCI, Mongolia NSO, Myanmar — no public API found (portal/xlsx only).
- Middle East: UAE FCSC, Qatar PSA, Jordan DoS, Kuwait CSB — portal-only; Saudi covered via national open-data portal.
- Latin America: Chile INE, Peru INEI, Ecuador INEC, Bolivia INE, Paraguay INE, Venezuela — no cell-level API (Socrata portals partially mitigate: Colombia yes; Chile/Peru datos abiertos portals exist but DANE-grade statistical APIs absent).
- Oceania: Pacific island NSOs (Fiji, PNG, Samoa...) — covered only via SPC SDD (sdd.spc.int, .Stat-based) — candidate for follow-up.
- Europe minor: Ukraine (Ukrstat — degraded by war), Belarus, Balkan NSOs beyond EU — not verified here.
- China NBS: biggest single-country gap — no official API at all.
- Census microdata: IPUMS (registered) remains the main harmonized route; most NSOs (India, Indonesia, Brazil) distribute microdata only via application/CD, not API.

Cross-ref: UNESCO UIS is placed in THIS category (demographics/education statistics); a pointer is kept in Category 24.

### Category notes

STATUS NOTES (discontinued / degraded / replaced — from wave-2 file 14):
- ReliefWeb: **API v1 DECOMMISSIONED** (verified HTTP 410) → v2 requires approved appname.
- IATI: **legacy datastore SOLR endpoint deprecated** → new api.iatistandard.org/datastore requires free subscription key (verified 401).
- UN Data (data.un.org): legacy SDMX-RI; bulk DownloadHandler still works (verified); prefer agency-native APIs.
- TurkStat, ITU, UNIDO, WIPO IPstats, DGBAS, NSO Thailand, GSO Vietnam: no official public API (search interface + bulk only). [TurkStat/DGBAS/NSO Thailand/GSO Vietnam blocks are in Category 23]

OpenFlights** | 2 OpenFlights (community) | 3 global | 4 airline routes/airports/airlines datasets | 5 aviation (English) | 6 https://openflights.org | 10 BULK DOWNLOAD (dat files) | 27 DEGRADED — openflights.org/data.html returns 404 [PROBE]; datasets unmaintained since ~2017; use GitHub mirrors (jpatokal/openflights) or OurAirports as replacement | 28 Tier E | 29 EASY | 34 superseded by OurAirports for airports

**43. Find a Grave** | 2 Ancestry.com | 3 US/global | 4 cemetery memorials | 5 genealogy (English) | 6 https://www.findagrave.com | 10 **NO API** (public API retired years ago); 403 bot-blocked [PROBE 403] | 27 OPERATIONAL as website only; commercial scraping prohibited | 28 Tier D | 29 HARD | 33 largest cemetery corpus but closed | 34 FamilySearch (cross-ref, has API)

### Category notes

#### REGIONAL COVERAGE SUMMARY (preserved from wave-2 file 16)
- **East Asia**: STRONG — Japan Search (national LOD graph), data.go.kr (huge API catalog), CBDB+CHGIS (China historical DH), Aozora (bulk), NCPSSD (free soc-sci). China mainstream scholarly (CNKI/Wanfang) remains API-closed.
- **South Asia**: MODERATE — Shodhganga OAI-PMH + data.gov.in verified but geo-blocked from sandbox; GRETIL excellent bulk. Indian Culture Portal (indianculture.gov.in) unreachable/no published API.
- **Middle East/Arabic**: WEAK machine access — Shamela bulk only; QDL IIIF but anti-bot; Ottoman Devlet Arşivleri has no API (search portal only, no verification achieved).
- **Latin America**: STRONG metadata infrastructure — LA Referencia + CLACSO OAI-PMH + DOAB harvest covers the region; AILLA for indigenous languages.
- **Russia/CIS**: WEAK — CyberLeninka/eLibrary live but no APIs; no open machine layer found.
- **Oceania/Pacific**: STRONG for language/culture (PARADISEC OAI, DigitalNZ API); Trove cross-ref.
- **Arctic**: STRONG — Arctic Data Center/DataONE REST + ONC Oceans 3.0 + GVP/NCEI mirrors.
- **Indigenous/endangered languages (global)**: STRONG discovery layer (OLAC in transition, PARADISEC, Pangloss, AILLA) + unique governance layer (Local Contexts API).
- **Specialized/rare**: STRONG — live-verified: OpenSky, SatNOGS, xeno-canto v3, Movebank, LibriVox, Gutenberg/Gutendex, Standard Ebooks, DOAB, OurAirports, Mushroom Observer, Wildlife Insights, GVP, Local Contexts.

#### GAPS / NOTES FOR LEAD (preserved from wave-2 file 16)
1. OLAC mid-migration (2025/26 rebuild) — re-verify after v2 relaunch.
2. xeno-canto v2 DISCONTINUED → v3 key-only (breaking change for existing integrations).
3. China: CNKI, Wanfang, CQVIP, NSSD all lack public APIs; NCPSSD is best free alternative. NGDC/CNCB (genomics) live [PROBE 200] but no documented public REST API — FTP/web bulk only; assigned to genomics agent for depth.
4. Geo-blocking observed: Shodhganga, data.gov.in, indianculture.gov.in, contents.koreanhistory.or.kr unreachable from sandbox egress — verify from in-region node before integration.
5. Russian-language machine access is the biggest structural gap: no OAI/API from CyberLeninka/eLibrary/RSL; may require partnership or permitted scraping.
6. Arabic/Ottoman: QDL blocks automation; Devlet Arşivleri (catalog.da.gov.tr) has no API — second structural gap.
7. Japanese national aggregator (Japan Search) bot-blocks datacenter IPs (403) — needs browser-like client or Japanese egress.
8. Not verified (time-boxed, recommend follow-up): NINJAL corpora (license-gated downloads, no API), FirstVoices (platform has internal API, no public docs found — community data sovereignty applies), MARKUS (tool not a source), eMammal, AISHub (free AIS feed w/ registration — plausible but unverified), Blitzortung (participant-only data access), Norwegian Polar Institute (api.npolar.no exists historically — unverified), Indian Culture Portal, Noorlib (Iranian journals — appears search-interface only), Biblioteca Digital Curt Nimuendajú (static site, no API).

Cross-refs: Japan Search full block is registered in Category 21 (file 13 B6); data.gov.in full block is registered in Category 23 (file 14 #35); DigitalNZ full block in Category 21 (file 13 B2).

# WAR ROOM OS — EARTH KNOWLEDGE BASE: MASTER SOURCE REGISTRY (2026)

Single consolidated registry of **every verified machine-accessible knowledge source** found by the discovery swarm (Wave 1 files 01–08 + Wave 2 files 09–16). Sources deduplicated across files into one row each; where a source spans two registry categories the primary category is shown with a secondary cross-reference, e.g. `16 (→21)`. Status-only / search-interface / commercial-gated entries are retained as valuable *negative findings* and are marked in the **2026 Status** column.

**Column conventions** — Access Type uses the block's protocol vocabulary (REST / GraphQL / SPARQL / OAI-PMH / IIIF / SDMX / OData / STAC / TAP / PXWEB / BULK / FEED / REPO / SEARCH INTERFACE; combos joined with `+`). Key Required?: Yes / No / Optional / Account-gated. Cost: Free / Freemium / Paid / Free-academic / Licensed. Tier: A–E (primary grade, combos as given). Difficulty: EASY / MODERATE / HARD (ranges with `–`). 2026 Status: OPERATIONAL / DEGRADED / DISCONTINUED / STATIC / SEARCH-ONLY / COMMERCIAL-GATED.

# WAR ROOM OS — EARTH KNOWLEDGE BASE REGISTRY: ANALYTICAL SECTIONS A–Z

Derived strictly from the 16 verified discovery reports (wave1 cats 01–08, wave2 cats 09–16). Category numbers in parentheses cite the source report. Where reports disagree, the disagreement is noted. Africa is covered by the separate Africa registry (`/mnt/agents/output/war_room_africa_api_registry.md`) and is cross-referenced, not duplicated.

## SECTION B — THE FIRST 25 TO INTEGRATE (optimal starter pack)

Selection: maximum cross-domain join value per integration hour; all free; 18 of 25 need no credential at all.

1. **Wikidata (15)** — the entity spine; every later source maps onto QIDs.
2. **Wikipedia REST API (15)** — instant multilingual summaries for any entity.
3. **NCBI E-utilities (04/08)** — one client unlocks PubMed, Taxonomy, ClinVar, GEO, SRA.
4. **Europe PMC (04)** — keyless full-text + citation graph for the biomedical layer.
5. **ClinicalTrials.gov v2 (04)** — keyless, structured, daily; the trial-entity backbone.
6. **openFDA (05)** — drugs, devices, recalls, adverse events in one keyless REST.
7. **PubChem (05)** — chemical entity resolution + xrefs to everything molecular.
8. **GBIF (07)** — species-occurrence ground truth; keyless reads.
9. **UniProt (08)** — protein ID mapping hub; async ID-mapping pattern reusable elsewhere.
10. **OSV.dev (02)** — one bulk zip + one API covers 30+ package ecosystems' vulnerabilities.
11. **NVD (02/03)** — CVE/CPE/CVSS canonical layer (optional key, free).
12. **GitHub API (01/02)** — repos, issues, releases, GHSA advisories with one token.
13. **CISA KEV (02/03)** — single-file bulk; the "actively exploited" flag for triage.
14. **arXiv (11)** — OAI-PMH + Atom; the preprint firehose pattern.
15. **OSM Overpass + Geofabrik (12)** — global POI/boundary queries + local planet index.
16. **GeoNames (12/15)** — free toponym resolution; geonameid becomes a core join key.
17. **Eurostat (14)** — SDMX-JSON pattern unlocks dozens of other statistical APIs later.
18. **US Census API (14)** — the US demographic ground truth; free key.
19. **FRED (10)** — 800k+ macro series through one free key.
20. **Congress.gov + GovInfo (10)** — US law machine layer; GovInfo doubles as bulk pattern.
21. **GLEIF (10)** — corporate identity resolution (LEI), keyless, daily golden copy.
22. **SEC EDGAR (10)** — company financials free with just a declared User-Agent.
23. **ORCID (15)** — person identity for the research graph; keyless public API.
24. **Internet Archive metadata + Wayback CDX (15)** — time travel + item metadata, keyless.
25. **ReliefWeb (14)** — real-time humanitarian events stream with a trivial appname registration.

Dependency note: 2, 12, 15–24 all emit identifiers (QID, PMID, RxCUI, DOI, geonameid, LEI, CIK, ORCID) that Sections U/T use as the router's primary keys; integrating these 25 first makes every subsequent wave cheaper.

## SECTION E — SOURCES NEEDING NO CREDENTIALS AT ALL

No signup, no key, no account for the primary read path (a declared User-Agent counts as no credential). Grouped by category.

**01 Software/coding:** PyPI; npm Registry; crates.io index/CDN/dumps; Maven Central solrsearch; MetaCPAN; RubyGems (read); ecosyste.ms; deps.dev; Homebrew Formulae JSON; Debian sources/UDD/snapshot; grep.app; IETF Datatracker/RFC Editor; MDN (GitHub content repo); kernel.org/lore (git/mbox/Atom); endoflife.date; Rosetta Code; DevDocs.
**02 Bugs/patches:** OSV.dev; CVE Services (read) + cvelistV5 git; CISA KEV; FIRST EPSS; Go VulnDB; MSRC CVRF; Ubuntu OVAL; Debian Security Tracker; Red Hat Security Data (Hydra); Apache Jira (anonymous read); Mozilla Bugzilla (public bugs); Launchpad (anonymous read); GitHub/GitLab public issues (token optional).
**03 Cybersecurity:** CVE Services/cvelistV5; CISA KEV; EPSS; MITRE ATT&CK (repo + TAXII); MITRE ATLAS; CWE/CAPEC zips; Exploit-DB git; MISP default feeds (most); CERT-FR feeds; JVN RSS; BSI WID RSS; NCSC-NL CSAF feed; Shodan InternetDB (keyless endpoint).
**04 Medical:** Europe PMC; PMC OA (FTP→AWS); ClinicalTrials.gov v2; EU CTIS (undocumented); EBI OLS4; Ontobee/HeGroup SPARQL; Orphanet Science tier; MedlinePlus (+Connect); Open Targets (GraphQL + MCP); WHO GHO OData.
**05 Pharma:** openFDA (key optional); RxNorm/RxNav/RxClass/Interactions; DailyMed; AccessGUDID; PubChem; ChEMBL; GtoPdb; PharmGKB; Health Canada DPD; BindingDB API; DrugCentral (bulk); Orange/Purple Book ZIPs; EMA medicines Excel; VAERS; WHO VigiAccess (search only).
**06 Traditional medicine:** COCONUT; LOTUS→Wikidata (WDQS); ChEBI 2.0; TCMSP/TCMID/SymMap/ETCM/IMPPAT (search/partial bulk); Dr. Duke's (bulk CSV); NAEB; PROTA4U; UTP; PFAF; SANCDB; WHO IRIS OAI; Wellcome Collection; KNApSAcK.
**07 Biology:** GBIF (reads); iNaturalist (reads); Catalogue of Life/ChecklistBank (reads); NCBI Taxonomy; WoRMS; ITIS; POWO (unofficial); Index Fungorum (search); BOLD v4; EOL + TraitBank; GloBI; PBDB; OBIS; GRIIS (GBIF/Zenodo); ICTV MSL; BacDive v2 (auth dropped Feb 2026); GlobalTreeSearch (form).
**08 Genomics:** NCBI Datasets v2; Ensembl REST; UniProt; RCSB PDB; AlphaFold DB; KEGG REST (academic, license-restricted); Reactome; WikiPathways; STRING; IntAct; OLS4; ENA; DDBJ; Cellosaurus; Metabolomics Workbench; MetaboLights (read); PRIDE; gnomAD.
**09 History/archaeology:** Open Context; ARIADNE; EDH; iDAI.gazetteer; CDLI; eBL (read); WHG (read); OpenHistoricalMap (read); Nomisma; Pleiades; PeriodO; ADS OAI; PANGAEA; Qatar Digital Library (IIIF — but see anti-bot note, 16); e-periodica; ANNO; Cliopatria; D-PLACE; Perseus/OGL; papyri.info; OpenITI; CBETA; Kanripo; GRETIL; EHRI (read); BDRC.
**10 Gov/law/econ:** legislation.gov.uk; Japan e-Gov Law API; Australia FRL OData; UK Gazette; Federal Register; Canada Justice Laws; Gesetze im Internet; WIPO Lex; UN Treaty Collection; NORMLEX/NATLEX; FAOLEX; India eGazette (search); OFAC SDN/SLS; UN SC list; UK OFSI; GLEIF; SEC EDGAR (UA only); Interpol notices (undocumented, degraded); USAspending; OCDS publishers; EU FTS; OpenSpending data (dormant); IDEA turnout; ECB SDW; BoE IADB; BoC Valet; BIS; BoJ CSV.
**11 Science/patents:** arXiv; INSPIRE-HEP; CERN Repository; HEPData; zbMATH; OEIS; PDG; NIST CODATA; NIST WebBook (search); NOMAD (read); OQMD; AFLOW; JARVIS (Figshare); ETSI/ITU-T (per-doc PDFs); EUIPO TMview (unofficial); INPI France open tier (recherche-entreprises.api.gouv.fr).
**12 Maps/space:** OSM Overpass/Nominatim/Planet/Geofabrik; Natural Earth; Overture Maps; Open-Meteo (non-commercial); api.weather.gov (UA); Met.no (UA); DWD Open Data; JMA XML feed; GEBCO; Swisstopo/PDOK/GA; CelesTrak; NOAA SWPC; JPL Horizons/SBDB (DEMO_KEY); MPC; NASA Exoplanet TAP; SIMBAD/VizieR; INPE STAC; USGS Landsat STAC/S3 (auth-free path).
**13 Academic/archives/museums:** OpenAIRE (read); OpenCitations (token optional); bioRxiv/medRxiv API; HAL; J-STAGE (non-commercial); EconStor; SciELO; CyberLeninka OAI; OAI-PMH IR ecosystem; NDL Search (casual); NDL IIIF; Japan Search; LIBRIS XL; Finna; NB Norway; datos.bne.es; CulturaItalia; Polona (IIIF); AtoM instances; museum APIs w/o key: AIC, Cleveland, V&A, SMK, Nasjonalmuseet, Wellcome, NG London.
**14 Statistics:** Eurostat; OECD SDMX; UN SDG; UN DESA WPP; UNHCR; UNESCO UIS; ADB KIDB; UNdata; OCHA FTS; AidData (bulk); UK ONS; Istat; INE Spain; CBS NL; SCB; SSB; StatBank DK; StatFin; GUS BDL (key optional); CZSO; eSankhyiki (undocumented); SingStat; data.gov.my; PSA OpenSTAT; ABS; Stats NZ; StatCan; IBGE SIDRA; Argentina Series API; Israel CBS; TurkStat (bulk); Saudi CKAN; Gapminder; GADM; Taiwan data.gov.tw CKAN.
**15 General/reference:** Wikidata; Wikipedia; Wikimedia Commons; Wikimedia dumps; DBpedia; YAGO; ConceptNet; Common Crawl; IA/Wayback (read); Kaikki; WordNet/OEWN; Glosbe (degraded); JMdict; CC-CEDICT; VIAF; ISNI; ORCID (public); ROR; lobid; MusicBrainz (read); Open Library; DBLP; Kiwix; FactGrid; Wikibase Cloud instances (read).
**16 Regional/specialized:** CBDB; Japan Search; Local Contexts (metadata read — key for API v2 per block); OpenSky (anonymous limited); SatNOGS; Mushroom Observer (GET); LibriVox; Gutenberg/Gutendex; Standard Ebooks; Arctic Data Center/DataONE; LA Referencia; CLACSO; Shodhganga OAI; DOAB; PARADISEC; OLAC; Pangloss; AILLA metadata; CHGIS; Aozora; OurAirports; Smithsonian GVP (bulk); al-Shamela (bulk).

## SECTION G — PAID / COMMERCIAL / LICENSED SOURCES (cost models as stated in blocks)

| Source | Cat | Cost model (from block) |
|--------|-----|--------------------------|
| DrugBank | 05 | PAID commercial (est. 5-figure+/yr, not public); free academic non-commercial per-project; free web checker reportedly retiring Mar 2026 (verify) |
| KEGG | 08 | PAID FTP subscription ~US$2,000–5,000/yr (bioinformatics.jp); commercial via Pathway Solutions; REST free academic but no redistribution |
| VulDB | 03 | PAID credits: 200/day tier; 10,000/day + USD 199/mo |

## SECTION P — UNIQUE SOURCES (no functional substitute anywhere else)

Per the blocks' own uniqueness claims:
- **Software Heritage** (01): only universal deduplicated source-code archive with persistent SWHIDs; recovers deleted code.
- **deps.dev** (01/02): computed resolved dependency graphs + hash→artifact forensics.
- **Debian snapshot** (01): distro-history time travel back to 2005.
- **endoflife.date** (01/02): only cross-vendor EOL/lifecycle normalization.
- **grep.app** (01): fastest free cross-repo regex code search (post-Sourcegraph).
- **Rosetta Code** (01): task×language matrix (1,200+ tasks × 900+ languages).
- **Homebrew JSON** (01): unique macOS package + install analytics.
- **CISA KEV** (02/03): only authoritative actively-exploited + ransomware-flag list.
- **EPSS** (03): only open exploit-prediction model across all CVEs.
- **Go VulnDB** (02): symbol-level affected-function data.
- **MSRC** (02): definitive CVE→KB→build mapping incl backports; **Red Hat Hydra** (02): backport-aware fixed versions; **Debian Tracker** (02): no-dsa triage rationales.
- **MITRE ATLAS** (03): only structured AI-adversary KB.
- **GreyNoise RIOT** (03): unique noise-vs-targeted + benign dataset; **MalwareBazaar** (03): only vetted confirmed-malware corpus with free samples.
- **WHO ICTRP** (04): only unifier of 17+ national trial registries (800k+).
- **WHO ICD API** (04): only official global morbidity/mortality coding system with API.
- **MedlinePlus Connect** (04): only consumer KB queryable by clinical codes.
- **Open Targets** (04): only open integrated target–disease–drug scored evidence graph.
- **GISAID** (04): largest curated respiratory-virus genomes (16M+, gated).
- **IHME GBD** (04): definitive comparable burden estimates (371 diseases, 204 countries).
- **AccessGUDID** (05): only authoritative US UDI device master data; **Health Canada DPD** (05): only official Canadian marketed-product feed; **VAERS** (05): only full-narrative open vaccine-safety corpus; **WHO VigiAccess** (05): only global cross-national ADR aggregation; **Orange Book** (05): patent/exclusivity + TE codes nowhere else open; **PharmGKB** (05): only machine-readable CPIC/DPWG dosing guidelines.
- **SymMap** (06): only structured TCM↔modern symptom bridge; **MPNS** (06): only authoritative multilingual medicinal-plant name authority; **TKDL** (06): world's largest codified Indian TM prior-art corpus (licensed); **SANCDB** (06): sole referenced South African NP set; **NAEB** (06): definitive indigenous N. American plant medicine.
- **BOLD** (07): only global curated DNA-barcode reference library; **GloBI** (07): only global open food-web/interaction graph; **PBDB** (07): only global fossil-occurrence graph; **GRIIS** (07): only expert-verified national invasive checklists; **ICTV** (07): sole official virus taxonomy; **IUCN RL** (07): only authoritative extinction-risk assessment; **TRY** (07): largest curated plant-trait compendium; **BacDive/LPSN** (07): only standardized bacterial phenotypes + official prokaryote nomenclature; **BirdLife IBA/KBA** (07): unique site layer (no API).
- **gnomAD** (08): largest population-frequency API; **AlphaFold DB** (08): only proteome-scale predictions; **Cellosaurus** (08): de-facto cell-line authority incl misidentified registry; **KEGG KO** (08): unique orthology + curated maps (license-restricted); **ORCS** (08): only large curated CRISPR-screen repo.
- **WHG** (09): global temporal gazetteer backbone; **PeriodO** (09): only cross-disciplinary period authority; **Cliopatria** (09): only open global polity-boundary time series; **Seshat** (09): largest expert-coded quantitative history; **impresso** (09): unique NLP stack over 40M+ historical newspaper articles; **NLI** (09): only API to global historical Jewish press; **EHRI** (09): unique transnational Holocaust-archive integration; **EDH** (09): largest open Latin epigraphy; **CDLI/eBL** (09): authoritative cuneiform KBs.
- **GLEIF** (10): only global LEI resolver with corporate trees; **USAspending** (10): award-level outlays + full DB dump; **TED** (10): complete EU procurement funnel; **Brazil CEIS/CEPIM/CEAF** (10): unique LatAm integrity registers; **IFES** (10): only global forward-looking election calendar API; **FRED/ALFRED** (10): vintage/revision data; **BIS** (10): unique international banking statistics; **UN Treaty Collection** (10): sole authoritative depositary record; **UN SC list** (10): baseline sanctions.
- **HEPData** (11): only machine-readable HEP plot data; **PDG** (11): authoritative particle world averages; **OEIS** (11): unique integer-sequence knowledge; **EPO OPS INPADOC** (11): best free global patent family/legal status; **TM-Link (IP Australia)** (11): cross-jurisdiction linked trademarks; **INPI patents-by-SIREN** (11): patent-to-company linkage; **SuperCon/NIMS** (11): unique superconductor corpus (unreachable legally).
- **Overture GERS** (12): stable global geo-entity UUIDs; **Mapillary** (12): only open global street-level imagery; **ONC** (16): unique cabled deep-sea observatory; **JAXA G-Portal** (12): only free global L-band SAR; **DISCOS** (12): physical properties of 40k+ space objects; **NASA Exoplanet Archive** (12): canonical exoplanet census w/ provenance; **Space-Track CDM** (12): only authoritative conjunction data; **JMA feed** (12): authoritative Japan early warning.
- **Trove** (13): Australian newspaper OCR full text; **Japan Search** (13/16): only cross-domain Japanese national portal; **NB Norway DH-lab** (13): full legal-deposit corpus incl broadcast; **KB Delpher** (13): best European bulk newspaper corpus w/ ALTO; **KISTI** (13): authoritative Korean S&T corpus.
- **UN DESA WPP** (14): canonical demographic projections; **UNHCR** (14): authoritative displacement incl statelessness; **IOM DTM** (14): subnational IDP near-real-time; **OCHA FTS** (14): only systematic humanitarian funding tracker; **AidData GCDF** (14): unique project-level Chinese development finance; **INEGI DENUE** (14): Mexican business microdirectory; **data.gov.my** (14): parliament/DUN-level Malaysian demographics.
- **Kaikki** (15): THE machine-readable Wiktionary; **BabelNet** (15): 600-language synset alignment; **FrameNet** (15): unique frame-semantic annotations; **ConceptNet** (15): multilingual commonsense incl CJK; **Common Crawl** (15): only open web-scale crawl; **IA/Wayback** (15): web time travel; **Diffbot** (15): largest continuously-crawled commercial KG.
- **Local Contexts Hub** (16): only machine-readable indigenous data-sovereignty labels; **OpenSky** (16): largest open ADS-B archive; **xeno-canto** (16): largest open bird-sound corpus; **Movebank** (16): unique open wildlife telemetry; **SatNOGS** (16): only open satellite-observation network API; **AILLA** (16): unique Amazonian/Mesoamerican community-controlled archive; **PARADISEC** (16): primary Pacific/Papuan recordings archive (1,366 languages); **Shodhganga** (16): unique Indian PhD mass; **CBDB+CHGIS** (16): pre-modern China bio/geo backbone; **Shamela** (16): largest classical Arabic corpus.
